# Mazama locality register

All 872 evidence records appear below. The five fits use 300 distinct localities. A range enters as one observation with both endpoints. The ledger records the original readings, preservation assumptions, coordinate precision and reasons for each decision. [Full locality decisions](principal-ranges/locality-decisions.csv) · [Model input](../../eruptions/Mazama_primary_ranges.csv) · [Field audit](principal-ranges/README.md)
